<script>




window.kim = window.kim || {};
kim.fullUserNameKey = "kim.kunde.fullUserName"
kim.fullUserNameMaxLength = 121
kim.isReloadFullName = function() {
var name = localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey]
return !name || name.length > kim.fullUserNameMaxLength
}
kim.updateUserFullName = function() {
if (localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] === "true" && kim.isReloadFullName()) {
$.get("/api/kundensvc/Kunde/Name/v1", function(data) {
if(data.length > kim.fullUserNameMaxLength) {
localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey] = ""
return
}
localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey] = data
kim.fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey])
}).fail(function() {
})
} else if (localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey]) {
kim.fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey])
}
}
kim.fillWelcome = function(value) {
$("#welcomeUser").html(value)
}

window.kim = window.kim || {};
kim.showAsLoggedInKey = "kim.login.showAsLoggedIn"
kim.updateLoginLinks = function() {
var loginLinks = $(".oLogin")
var logoutLinks = $(".oLogout")
kim.onLogout(logoutLinks)
kim.processLoginState(loginLinks, logoutLinks)
}
kim.processLoginState = function(loginLinks, logoutLinks) {
try {
if (localStorage) {
if (localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] === "true") {
loginLinks.hide()
logoutLinks.show()
} else {
logoutLinks.hide()
loginLinks.show()
}
}
} catch(storageError) {}
}
kim.onLogout = function(logoutLinks) {
logoutLinks.on("click.kim", function (event) {
localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] = false
localStorage.removeItem(kim.fullUserNameKey)
kim.fillWelcome("")
kim.setzeClubstatusKeinMitglied()
document.cookie = "cclogin-data=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC; path=/;";
})
}

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        $(document).ready(function () {
            var _href = '/filiale/headerfiliale/sap';
            var _headerFilialeId = ThaliaApp.util.getCookie('headerfiliale');

            if (_headerFilialeId === '') {
                requestHeaderFiliale(_href);
            } else {
                ThaliaApp.util.delCookie('red_lieblingsfiliale_not_available');
                $.when($.ajax(_href + "/" + _headerFilialeId))
                    .done(function (resp) {
                        var _headerfiliale = $("#headerfiliale");
                        _headerfiliale.replaceWith(resp);
                   });
            }

            if (window.trackData !== 'undefined' && typeof window.trackData.refreshTracking === 'function') {
                window.trackData.refreshTracking();
            }
        });

        function requestHeaderFiliale (href) {
            if (ThaliaApp.util.getCookie('red_lieblingsfiliale_not_available') === 'true') {
                // 
                return;
            }
            try {
                var _isLoggedIn = localStorage.getItem('kim.login.showAsLoggedIn');
            } catch(e) {
                return;
            }
            if (_isLoggedIn === 'true') {
                $.when($.ajax({url: href, headers: {'Accept': 'text/html'}}))
                    .done(function (resp) {
                        var _headerfiliale = $("#headerfiliale");
                        _headerfiliale.replaceWith(resp);
                    })
                    .fail(function (jqXHR) {
                        if(jqXHR.status === 404) {
                            ThaliaApp.util.setCookie('red_lieblingsfiliale_not_available', 'true')
                        }
                    });
            }
        }

    </script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    var geolocationOptions = {
        enableHighAccuracy: false,
        timeout: 30000,  // milliseconds (30 seconds)
        maximumAge: 600000 // milliseconds (10 minutes)
    };

    function getGeolocationForHeader() {
        var deferred = $.Deferred();
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
            function (position) {
                deferred.resolve({
                    longitude: position.coords.longitude,
                    latitude: position.coords.latitude
                });
            },
            function (error) {
                deferred.reject(searchByGeoLocationOff());
            },
            geolocationOptions
        );
        return deferred.promise();
    }


    function searchByGeoLocation() {

        if (window.trackData !== 'undefined' && typeof window.trackData.sendinfo === 'function') {
            window.trackData.sendinfo({linkId: 'naechsteFilialeFinden'});
        }

        $.when(getGeolocationForHeader()).then(
            function (position) {
                window.location = '/shop/home/filialen/showStadt/?geo=' + position.longitude + "," + position.latitude;
            }
        );
        return false;
    }

    function searchByGeoLocationOff() {
        window.location = '/shop/home/filialen';
        return false;
    }


</script>

<script language="JavaScript">

    (function () {
        window.kim = window.kim || {};
        var showAsLoggedInKey = kim.showAsLoggedInKey || 'kim.login.showAsLoggedIn';
        var fullUserNameKey = "kim.kunde.fullUserName";
        var fullUserNameMaxLength = 121;
        var logoutEventIdentifier = kim.logoutEventIdentifier || 'kim.logout'

        function isReloadFullName() {
            var name = localStorage[fullUserNameKey];
            return !name || name.length > fullUserNameMaxLength
        }

        function updateUserFullName() {
            if (localStorage[showAsLoggedInKey] === "true" && isReloadFullName()) {
                fetch("/api/kundensvc/Kunde/Name/v1").then(function (response) {
                    return response.text();
                }).then(function (kundenname) {
                    if (kundenname.length > fullUserNameMaxLength) {
                        localStorage[fullUserNameKey] = "";
                        return
                    }
                    localStorage[fullUserNameKey] = kundenname;
                    fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[fullUserNameKey]);
                })
            }
            if (localStorage[fullUserNameKey]) {
                fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[fullUserNameKey]);
            }
        }

        function fillWelcome(value) {
            document.getElementById("welcomeUser").innerText = value;
        }

        updateUserFullName();

        window.addEventListener(logoutEventIdentifier,
            function() {
                localStorage.removeItem(fullUserNameKey);
                fillWelcome("");
            })
    })();

</script>

<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
    kim = kim || {}
    kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey = kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey || "kim.merkzettel.artikelanzahl"
    kim.showAsLoggedInKey = kim.showAsLoggedInKey || "kim.login.showAsLoggedIn"

    kim.renderArtikelanzahl = function(artikelAnzahl) {
        var merkzettelHeaderElements = document.getElementsByTagName("merkzettel-header");
        if(merkzettelHeaderElements.length == 0){
            if (artikelAnzahl && artikelAnzahl != "0") {
                $("#merkzettel-burger-link").html("Merkzettel (" + artikelAnzahl + ")");
                $(".merkzettel-header-link").html("Merkzettel (" + artikelAnzahl + ")");
            }else{
                $("#merkzettel-burger-link").html("Merkzettel");
                $(".merkzettel-header-link").html("Merkzettel");
            }
        }else{
            for( var i = 0; i<merkzettelHeaderElements.length; i++){
                merkzettelHeaderElements[i].renderArtikelanzahl(artikelAnzahl);
            }
        }
    }

    kim.updateArtikelanzahl = function() {
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            if (!(localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] === "true")) {
                kim.clearArtikelanzahl();
                kim.renderArtikelanzahl("0");
                return;
            }

            if(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]) {
                kim.renderArtikelanzahl(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]);
                return;
            }

            $.get("/api/rest/public/merkzettel/admin/artikelanzahl/v1", function (data) {
                        localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey] = data;
                        kim.renderArtikelanzahl(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]);
            }).fail(function () {
                kim.clearArtikelanzahl();
            })

        })
    }

    kim.incrementArtikelanzahl = function(){
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            if (localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]) {
                var neueArtikelanzahl = parseInt(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]) + 1;
                localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey] = neueArtikelanzahl;
                kim.renderArtikelanzahl(neueArtikelanzahl)
            }
        })
    }

    kim.clearAndUpdateArtikelanzahl = function() {
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            kim.clearArtikelanzahl();
            kim.updateArtikelanzahl();
        })
    }

    kim.clearArtikelanzahl = function(){
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            localStorage.removeItem(kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey);
        })
    }

    kim.withLocalStorage = function(callback) {
        try {
            if (localStorage) {
                callback();
            }
        } catch (storageError) {}
    }
</script>

<script>
        fetch(
            '/api/warenkorb/size',
            {
                method: 'GET',
                cache: 'no-store',
                headers:
                    {
                        'Accept': 'application/json'
                    },
                credentials: 'include'
            }
        )
            .then(function (response) {
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('Request: ' + response.url + ' Status Code: ' + response.status);
                }

                return response.json();
            })
            .then(function (responseJson) {
                if (responseJson.anzahlArtikel < 1) {
                    return;
                }
                document.getElementById('artikelAnzahlWarenkorb').innerHTML = responseJson.anzahlArtikel;
            })
            .catch(function (err) {
                var trackData = window.trackData;
                if(trackData) {
                    trackData.sendinfo({}, 'WarenkorbsymbolFetchError', 'click');
                }
            })
    </script>

<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
    // Inputfeld mit Loginname vorausfüllen, wenn vorhanden
    $(function () {
        var $newsletterInput = $('#input-NewsletterAnmelden');
        if (localStorage && $newsletterInput.val() === "") {
            $newsletterInput.val(localStorage["kim.login.loginUsername"])
        }
    })

    // Newsletter-Link unter Mein Konto
    $('a[href="#ocsNewsletterContainer"]').click(function(event) {
        // Klick auf den Link tracken
        window.trackData.sendinfo({}, "Mein_Konto_Newsletter_bestellen", "click")

        // Inputfeld fokussieren, nachdem Newsletter-Link geklickt wurde
        $('input#input-NewsletterAnmelden').focus()
        event.preventDefault()
    })

    // Inputfeld fokussieren, wenn beim Laden der Newsletter-Anker in der Location ist (z.B. Newsletter aus CH-Mail)
    if (location.hash === "#ocsNewsletterContainer") {
        $('input#input-NewsletterAnmelden').focus()
    }
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    var $callout = $("#callout-generalMessageNewsletter");

    if($callout && $callout.is(":visible")) {
        // Erfolg der Anmeldung tracken
        if ($callout.hasClass("success")) {
            window.trackData.sendinfo({linkId: "Newsletter_bestellen_Erfolg"});
        }

        // Fehler bei Anmeldung tracken
        if ($callout.hasClass("alert")) {
            window.trackData.add({
                "customParameter": {
                    15: ""   // Fehler bei Newsletter-Anmeldung
                }
            }, {merge: true})
        }
    }

    // Button-Klick tracken
    $("button[data-test='subscribeNewsletter-button']").click(function (event) {
        window.trackData.sendinfo({}, "Newsletter_bestellen", "click");
    });
</script>

(function() {
    /* Selective Attribute DataLayer Library Tag */
    var _, allAttr, allowedList, attr, attributes, dataLayerIngester, dataObj,
        isAllowed, keepCase, libUtil, omitKeys, pageAttr, prefix, toSet, trim,
        userAttr, util, value,
        hasProp = {}.hasOwnProperty;
    _ = Krux('require:underscore');
    util = Krux('require:util');
    libUtil = Krux('require:util.library-tag');
    dataLayerIngester = Krux('require:scrape').ingestDataLayer;

    /* Safe copy of dataLayer object */
    dataObj = Krux('scrape.javascript', 'dataLayer');

    /* String trimming helper function */
    trim = function(attr) {
        return ("" + attr).replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, '');
    };

    /* Attribute configs */
    pageAttr = _.map('dmp.basket.product.cat_path,dmp.basket.product.author,dmp.basket.product.sale,dmp.basket.product.language,dmp.basket.product.verkaufsrang,dmp.basket.product.rating,dmp.basket.product.fixedprice,dmp.product.fixedprice,dmp.basket.product.form,dmp.product.form,dmp.basket.product.sale,dmp.cat0,dmp.cat1,dmp.cat2,dmp.cat3,dmp.pagetype,dmp.product.cat,dmp.product.ean,dmp.product.brand,dmp.product.name,dmp.product.price,dmp.basket.size,dmp.basket.value,dmp.basket.product.ean,dmp.basket.product.name,dmp.basket.product.cat,dmp.basket.product.price,dmp.checkout.payment.method,dmp.checkout.product.ean,dmp.checkout.product.name,dmp.checkout.product.cat,dmp.checkout.product.price,dmp.checkout.total,dmp.search.term,dmp.pagetitle,dmp.product.availability,dmp.product.author,dmp.product.verkaufsrang,dmp.product.rating,dmp.wishlist.size,dmp.wishlist.value,dmp.wishlist.product.ean,dmp.wishlist.product.name,dmp.wishlist.product.cat,dmp.wishlist.product.price,dmp.checkout.quantity,dmp.basket.quantity,dmp.checkout.size,dmp.product.availability,dmp.product.sale,dmp.product.catpath,dmp.product.language'.split(','), trim);
    userAttr = _.map('dmp.user.clubstatus,dmp.user.age,dmp.user.gender,dmp.user.postalcode,dmp.user.city,dmp.user.devicetype'.split(','), trim);

    /* Create a array of attributes striping any empty strings */
    allAttr = _.without(pageAttr.concat(userAttr), '');

    /* Configuration settings */
    keepCase = 'true' === 'true';
    omitKeys = 'undefined'.split(',');

    /* Resolve Prefix */
    prefix = libUtil.resolvePrefix('text', 'tat',
        'undefined');

    /* Function to varify if attribute should be used */
    isAllowed = function(value, whitelist) {
        var i, len, str, x;
        str = "" + value;
        if (!((value != null) && str.length > 0)) {
            return false;
        }
        for (i = 0, len = whitelist.length; i < len; i++) {
            x = whitelist[i];
            if (value.match(x) != null) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    };

    /* Get a full list of attributes usting the dataLayer tool */
    attributes = dataLayerIngester(dataObj, {
        omitKeys: libUtil.removeFalsyStrings(omitKeys.concat(libUtil.EXCLUDE_KEYS_CONFIG)),
        omitValues: libUtil.EXCLUDE_VALUES_CONFIG,
        caseSensitive: keepCase,
        useFullPath: 'true' === 'true',
        useLastValue: 'false' === 'true',
        customDelimited: [/./],
        altDelimiter: ',',
        userKeys: _.map(userAttr, function(exp) {
            return new RegExp("(^|\\.)" + exp + "$");
        }),
        optimizeNames: true
    });

    /* Only set Attributes in the allowed list */
    allowedList = _.map(allAttr, function(name) {
        return new RegExp("(_attr_|_attr_" + prefix + "|\\.)" + (keepCase ?
            name : libUtil.normalizeAttrName(name, {
                removeDot: false
            })) + "$");
    });
    toSet = {};
    for (attr in attributes) {
        if (!hasProp.call(attributes, attr)) continue;
        value = attributes[attr];
        if (isAllowed(attr, allowedList)) {
            toSet[attr] = value;
        }
    }
    toSet = Krux('prefix:attr', toSet, prefix);
    Krux('set', toSet);
}).call();

(function(){
    var algorithm, id, idPath, idSource, idType;

    // Add type as variable in case other types are required
    idType = 'email';

    // Fixed list of allowed algorithm type
    algorithm = 'sha256';

    // Scrape methods for id collection
    idSource = 'dataLayer';

    idPath = 'dmp.user.email';
    id = Krux('scrape.' + idSource, idPath);

    // We should not proceed if we collect a bad value e.g. non-string
    if (typeof id !== 'string') return;

    // If the id looks like an email, hash with sha256
    if (/^.+@./.test(id)) {
        algorithm = 'sha256';
        id = Krux('scrape.email_sha256', id);
    }

    if (id) Krux('set', "user_attr_" + idType + "_" + algorithm, id);

})();

// Salesforce DMP Transaction Pixel
!function(){

    var KX_TRANSACTION_CONFIG = {
        // dataLayerName: '',
        transactionURLSubstr: 'none',
        transactionQtyMacro: 'dmp.checkout.quantity',
        transactionPriceMacro: 'dmp.checkout.total',
        transactionIdentifier: 'dmp.pagetype:purchase',
        transactionFields: 'dmp.checkout.product.cat,dmp.checkout.payment.method,dmp.checkout.site,dmp.checkout.instorepickup'.split(',')
    };

    var ecommerceDataLayerScraper = {

        _config: {},
        _debug: !!Krux('scrape.url_param', 'dtcdebug'),
        _firePixel: Krux('require:http').pixel,

        _log: function(msg) {
            return this._debug && console && console.debug('KX DTC: ' + msg.toString());
        },

        init: function() {
            this._config = KX_TRANSACTION_CONFIG;
            this._log('Scraper intialized');
            return this;
        },

        getValueByMacro: function(macro, dfault) {
           return (Krux('scrape.dataLayer', macro) || '').toString() || dfault;
        },

        getOrderDate: function() {
            // returns UTC date in yyyy-mm-dd format
            var d = new Date();
            function pad(n) { return n<10 ? '0' + n : n; }
            return d.getUTCFullYear() +
                '-' + pad(d.getUTCMonth()+1) +
                '-' + pad(d.getUTCDate());
        },

        getOrderPrice: function() {
            return this.getValueByMacro(this._config.transactionPriceMacro) || 0;
        },

        getOrderQuantity: function() {
            return this.getValueByMacro(this._config.transactionQtyMacro) || 0;
        },

        isTransactionComplete: function() {
            var c = this._config,
                _id = c.transactionIdentifier.split(':')[0],
                _val = c.transactionIdentifier.split(':')[1] || '';

            // Are we on a confirmation page url?
            if(c.transactionURLSubstr && c.transactionURLSubstr !== 'none' && ~location.pathname.toLowerCase().indexOf(c.transactionURLSubstr.toLowerCase())) {
                return true;
            }
            // is a transaction identified by the existence of a GTM macro?
            else if(_id && _id !== 'none' && !!scraper.getValueByMacro(_id) && !_val) {
                return true;
            }
            // is a transaction identified by the existence of a GTM macro and a specific value?
            // non-strict comparison against value to allow for numbers entered in form as strings
            else if(_id && _id !== 'none' && !!scraper.getValueByMacro(_id) && scraper.getValueByMacro(_id) == _val) {
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        },

        trackTransaction: function(data) {
            this._log('Firing transaction pixel...');
            data._kpid  = Krux('get', 'pubid');
            data.source = Krux('get', 'site');
            this._firePixel({
                url: '//beacon.krxd.net/transaction.gif',
                data: data
            });
        }
    };

    var scraper = ecommerceDataLayerScraper.init();

    data = {
        date: scraper.getOrderDate(),
        price: scraper.getOrderPrice(),
        quantity: scraper.getOrderQuantity()
    };

    KX_TRANSACTION_CONFIG.transactionFields.map(function(e){
        var _name = e.split(':')[0];
        var _macro = e.split(':')[1] || _name;
        data[_name] = scraper.getValueByMacro(_macro);
    });

    // fire transaction.gif if on confirmation page
    return scraper.isTransactionComplete() && scraper.trackTransaction(data);

}();
